- **arfiti-account-choice**: Choose the ledger account for one purchase in Arfiti - a card charge the bank agent posts or a line of a supplier bill - from the records, in a fixed order of authority (client rule, vendor default, vendor history, vendor notes, the chart of accounts), and say which evidence decided it. - **arfiti-bank-statement-lines**: Settle one bank account's (or card programme's) unreconciled statement lines in Arfiti through the MCP tools - confirm a payment-batch line, post a bank entry or payment through submit anchored to the statement line and match it, or exclude a duplicate - in a fixed order of checks, never breaking a tie with a date or an amount, and reading the line back after every write. Use for every bank session. ([arfiti-technologies/arfiti-plugins](https://github.com/arfiti-technologies/arfiti-plugins/tree/HEAD/arfiti-core-agent-procedures/skills/arfiti-bank-statement-lines) · ★5)
- **arfiti-bill-processing**: Process one incoming vendor bill in Arfiti end to end through the MCP tools only - read the document, find or delegate the vendor, choose accounts and tax codes from vendor defaults, history and the organisation's rules, check for a duplicate, validate, post through submit, attach the source document, and report. Use for every bill session. ([arfiti-technologies/arfiti-plugins](https://github.com/arfiti-technologies/arfiti-plugins/tree/HEAD/arfiti-core-agent-procedures/skills/arfiti-bill-processing) · ★5)
- **arfiti-billing-usage-answers**: Turn a person's answer to a "provide usage data" task into recorded contract usage and a billing run for exactly those contracts, through the Arfiti MCP tools - read the task and the answer, map each figure to a metered contract line, record it with submit("contract_usage", "record"), then submit("billing_run", "execute") for those contracts. Never invents a quantity. Use when a usage-data task has been answered. - **argument-reading**: 论证精读（Argument Reading）：把一篇完整的新闻、长评、政策解读、宣传文案或观点长文，重建为可审查的论证地图，判断它可不可信、强在哪、断点在哪、藏了什么前提。核心方法是先善意重建作者最强版本的论证（钢人原则），再评价证据、推理、隐藏前提与边界，并核查会改变核心判断的关键事实。触发：「这篇文章可信吗」「帮我精读/解读一下这篇」「拆一下它的论证和证据」「帮我自检这篇文章的论证漏洞」「转发/引用/反驳前帮我把关」「用批判眼光看看这篇」。不适用：只核查一条离散说法或数据（走 claim-check）、两个方案怎么选（走 steelman-choice）、拆解优秀成品学做法（走 reverse-teardown）、学术论文评审（用专门的学术评审方法）、只要摘要/翻译/润色、要求站队或煽动情绪。 ([Job-Yang/jobyang-ai-skills](https://github.com/Job-Yang/jobyang-ai-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/argument-reading) · ★103)
